#include "page.hpp"

namespace sx
{

void apply_page_backdrop(hui::gfx::BackdropSpec &backdrop, float time)
{
    backdrop.mode = hui::gfx::BackdropMode::gradient;
    backdrop.colors[0] = theme::ground;
    backdrop.colors[1] = theme::ground;
    backdrop.colors[2] = theme::accent_deep;
    backdrop.params[0] = 0.92f;
    backdrop.params[1] = 0.0f;
    backdrop.params[2] = 0.55f;
    backdrop.time = time;
}

} // namespace sx


===== FILE: ui/page.hpp =====
// What every page of the left rail has in common: the rail and its focus,
// the entrance clock, the violet page background.

#pragma once

#include "chrome.hpp"
#include "content.hpp"
#include "core/input.hpp"
#include "gfx/backdrop_spec.hpp"

namespace sx
{

// The page background: near-black with Stremio's violet light.
void apply_page_backdrop(hui::gfx::BackdropSpec &backdrop, float time);

class PageBase
{
  public:
    virtual ~PageBase() = default;

    // The page opened: its entrance restarts, and the cursor is on its content. The rail
    // is not left holding the focus it had when the page was last left.
    void enter()
    {
        age_ = 0.0f;
        rail_.reset(page_);
    }
    bool rail_focused() const
    {
        return rail_.focused();
    }
    // The page under the rail's cursor, as an index into Page.
    int rail_cursor() const
    {
        return rail_.cursor();
    }

  protected:
    PageBase(const Env &env, Page page) : env_(env), page_(page)
    {
        rail_.set_page(page);
    }

    // While the rail holds the focus it takes the D-pad: up/down choose a
    // page, right hands the focus back. True when it used `input`.
    bool rail_input(const hui::InputFrame &input)
    {
        if (!rail_.focused())
            return false;
        if (input.nav == hui::Direction::up)
            rail_.move(-1);
        else if (input.nav == hui::Direction::down)
            rail_.move(1);
        else if (input.nav == hui::Direction::right)
            rail_.focus(false);
        return true;
    }
    void to_rail()
    {
        rail_.focus(true);
    }
    void tick(float dt)
    {
        age_ += dt;
        clock_ += dt;
        rail_.set_page(page_);
        rail_.update(dt);
    }

    const Env &env_;
    Page page_;
    Rail rail_;
    float age_ = 0.0f;
    float clock_ = 0.0f;
};

} // namespace sx


===== FILE: ui/player.cpp =====
#include "player.hpp"

#include "cards.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>

namespace sx
{

using hui::gfx::Align;
using hui::gfx::Color;
using hui::gfx::Rect;
using hui::ui::text;
namespace tween = hui::tween;

namespace
{

std::string clock_text(double seconds)
{
    const long total = static_cast<long>(std::max(0.0, seconds));
    char out[24];
    if (total >= 3600)
        std::snprintf(out, sizeof(out), "%ld:%02ld:%02ld", total / 3600, total / 60 % 60, total % 60);
    else
        std::snprintf(out, sizeof(out), "%ld:%02ld", total / 60, total % 60);
    return out;
}

// Wraps one cue line to the subtitle width: at most two lines, the second
// ellipsized if text remains. Splits on ASCII spaces, which never appear
// inside a UTF-8 sequence; overlong single words are ellipsized by the font.
// Thai (U+0E00-U+0E7F) has no spaces: long Thai lines are wrapped by
// character instead of being truncated.
static std::size_t utf8_char_len(const char *p)
{
    unsigned char c = static_cast<unsigned char>(p[0]);
    if (c < 0x80)
        return 1;
    if (c < 0xE0)
        return 2;
    if (c < 0xF0)
        return 3;
    return 4;
}

static bool is_thai_char(const char *p, std::size_t remaining)
{
    if (remaining < 3)
        return false;
    unsigned char b0 = static_cast<unsigned char>(p[0]);
    unsigned char b1 = static_cast<unsigned char>(p[1]);
    unsigned char b2 = static_cast<unsigned char>(p[2]);
    return b0 == 0xE0 && (b1 == 0xB8 || b1 == 0xB9) && b2 >= 0x80 && b2 <= 0xBF;
}

// Decodes one UTF-8 codepoint. Caller must ensure the bytes are valid.
static std::uint32_t utf8_codepoint(const char *p)
{
    unsigned char c0 = static_cast<unsigned char>(p[0]);
    if (c0 < 0x80)
        return c0;
    if (c0 < 0xE0)
        return (static_cast<std::uint32_t>(c0 & 0x1F) << 6) | (p[1] & 0x3F);
    if (c0 < 0xF0)
        return (static_cast<std::uint32_t>(c0 & 0x0F) << 12) | ((static_cast<std::uint32_t>(p[1] & 0x3F) << 6)) |
               (p[2] & 0x3F);
    return (static_cast<std::uint32_t>(c0 & 0x07) << 18) | ((static_cast<std::uint32_t>(p[1] & 0x3F) << 12)) |
           ((static_cast<std::uint32_t>(p[2] & 0x3F) << 6)) | (p[3] & 0x3F);
}

// Thai combining marks: must not start a line (they anchor to the previous
// base consonant). U+0E31, U+0E34-U+0E3A (vowels), U+0E47-U+0E4E (tone marks).
static bool is_thai_combining_mark(std::uint32_t cp)
{
    return cp == 0x0E31 || (cp >= 0x0E34 && cp <= 0x0E3A) || (cp >= 0x0E47 && cp <= 0x0E4E);
}

// Thai leading vowels (U+0E40-U+0E44): written before the consonant, must not
// end a line (they would strand without their base).
static bool is_thai_leading_vowel(std::uint32_t cp)
{
    return cp >= 0x0E40 && cp <= 0x0E44;
}

// Splits text into grapheme clusters for Thai: a base character plus its
// combining marks, with leading vowels kept together with their base.
// Non-Thai characters are single-char clusters.
static void split_thai_clusters(const std::string &text, std::vector<std::string> &out)
{
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < text.size();)
    {
        std::size_t cl = utf8_char_len(text.data() + i);
        if (i + cl > text.size())
            cl = text.size() - i;
        std::uint32_t cp = utf8_codepoint(text.data() + i);
        std::string cluster = text.substr(i, cl);
        i += cl;
        // A leading vowel must stay with the following base character.
        if (cp >= 0x0E00 && cp <= 0x0E7F && is_thai_leading_vowel(cp) && i < text.size())
        {
            cl = utf8_char_len(text.data() + i);
            if (i + cl > text.size())
                cl = text.size() - i;
            cluster += text.substr(i, cl);
            i += cl;
        }
        // Attach following combining marks to the cluster.
        while (i < text.size())
        {
            cl = utf8_char_len(text.data() + i);
            if (i + cl > text.size())
                break;
            std::uint32_t ncp = utf8_codepoint(text.data() + i);
            if (ncp < 0x0E00 || ncp > 0x0E7F || !is_thai_combining_mark(ncp))
                break;
            cluster += text.substr(i, cl);
            i += cl;
        }
        out.push_back(cluster);
    }
}

void wrap_subtitle_line(const hui::gfx::Font *font, const std::string &text, float size, float max_w,
                        std::vector<std::string> &out)
{
    if (text.empty() || font->measure(text, size) <= max_w)
    {
        out.push_back(text);
        return;
    }
    // Thai path: no spaces in Thai, so wrap by grapheme cluster. Breaking
    // between clusters (never before a combining mark, never after a leading
    // vowel) keeps marks anchored to their base consonant.
    bool has_thai = false;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < text.size();)
    {
        if (is_thai_char(text.data() + i, text.size() - i))
        {
            has_thai = true;
            break;
        }
        i += utf8_char_len(text.data() + i);
    }
    if (has_thai && text.find(' ') == std::string::npos)
    {
        std::vector<std::string> clusters;
        split_thai_clusters(text, clusters);
        std::size_t ci = 0;
        for (int row = 0; row < 2 && ci < clusters.size(); ++row)
        {
            std::string line;
            while (ci < clusters.size())
            {
                const std::string trial = line + clusters[ci];
                if (!line.empty() && font->measure(trial, size) > max_w)
                    break;
                line = trial;
                ++ci;
            }
            if (row == 1 && ci < clusters.size())
            {
                // Second line overflow: ellipsize line + remainder.
                std::string rest;
                for (; ci < clusters.size(); ++ci)
                    rest += clusters[ci];
                line = font->fit(line + rest, size, max_w);
                ci = clusters.size();
            }
            out.push_back(line);
        }
        return;
    }
    std::vector<std::string> words;
    for (std::size_t s = 0; s < text.size();)
    {
        std::size_t e = text.find(' ', s);
        if (e == std::string::npos)
            e = text.size();
        if (e > s)
            words.push_back(text.substr(s, e - s));
        s = e + 1;
    }
    std::size_t wi = 0;
    for (int row = 0; row < 2 && wi < words.size(); ++row)
    {
        std::string line;
        while (wi < words.size())
        {
            const std::string trial = line.empty() ? words[wi] : line + " " + words[wi];
            if (font->measure(trial, size) > max_w)
                break;
            line = trial;
            ++wi;
        }
        if (line.empty() && wi < words.size())
        {
            // A single word wider than max_w: if it contains Thai, break it
            // by grapheme cluster instead of truncating (mixed Thai/English
            // cues like "สวัสดีครับ John" would otherwise lose text).
            bool word_has_thai = false;
            for (std::size_t k = 0; k < words[wi].size();)
            {
                if (is_thai_char(words[wi].data() + k, words[wi].size() - k))
                {
                    word_has_thai = true;
                    break;
                }
                k += utf8_char_len(words[wi].data() + k);
            }
            if (word_has_thai)
            {
                std::vector<std::string> clusters;
                split_thai_clusters(words[wi], clusters);
                std::string part;
                std::size_t used = 0;
                for (; used < clusters.size(); ++used)
                {
                    const std::string trial = part + clusters[used];
                    if (!part.empty() && font->measure(trial, size) > max_w)
                        break;
                    part = trial;
                }
                if (part.empty())
                {
                    // Even one cluster doesn't fit: fall back to fit().
                    part = font->fit(words[wi], size, max_w);
                    ++wi;
                }
                else if (used < clusters.size())
                {
                    // Push the remainder back as a new word for the next row.
                    std::string rest;
                    for (; used < clusters.size(); ++used)
                        rest += clusters[used];
                    words[wi] = rest;
                    // Don't advance wi; the remainder will be processed next.
                }
                else
                {
                    ++wi;
                }
                line = part;
            }
            else
            {
                line = font->fit(words[wi], size, max_w);
                ++wi;
            }
        }
        if (row == 1 && wi < words.size())
        {
            std::string tail = line;
            for (; wi < words.size(); ++wi)
                tail += " " + words[wi];
            line = font->fit(tail, size, max_w);
        }
        out.push_back(line);
    }
}

} // namespace

void draw_outlined_text(hui::gfx::DrawList &list, const hui::ui::FontRef &font, const std::string &value, float x,
                        float baseline, float size, Color colour, Align align)
{
    const float r = std::max(2.0f, size * 0.055f);
    static const float kDir[8][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}, {0.7f, 0.7f}, {-0.7f, 0.7f}, {0.7f, -0.7f}, {-0.7f, -0.7f}};
    for (const auto &d : kDir)
        text(list, font, value, x + d[0] * r, baseline + d[1] * r, size, Color::rgb(0x000000, 0.92f * colour.a), align);
    text(list, font, value, x, baseline, size, colour, align);
}

void PlayerUi::snap()
{
    controls_.snap(state.controls ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
    menu_.snap(state.menu ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
    paused_.snap(state.paused ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
    buffering_.snap(state.buffering >= 0 ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
    upnext_.snap(!state.upnext_title.empty() ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
    binge_.snap(state.binge_prompt ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
    skip_.snap(!state.skip_prompt.empty() ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
    skip_y_.snap(state.upnext_title.empty() ? 688.0f : 548.0f);
}

void PlayerUi::update(float dt)
{
    clock_ += dt;
    controls_.target = state.controls || state.paused || state.menu ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
    controls_.update(dt, 16.0f);
    menu_.target = state.menu ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
    menu_.update(dt, 18.0f);
    paused_.target = state.paused ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
    paused_.update(dt, 20.0f);
    buffering_.target = state.buffering >= 0 ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
    buffering_.update(dt, 14.0f);
    upnext_.target = !state.upnext_title.empty() ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
    upnext_.update(dt, 18.0f);
    binge_.target = state.binge_prompt ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
    binge_.update(dt, 18.0f);
    skip_.target = !state.skip_prompt.empty() ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
    skip_.update(dt, 18.0f);
    skip_y_.target = state.upnext_title.empty() ? 688.0f : 548.0f;
    skip_y_.update(dt, 14.0f);
}

void PlayerUi::draw_subtitle(hui::gfx::DrawList &list) const
{
    if (state.line.empty())
        return;
    const float size = state.sub_size == 0 ? 42.0f : state.sub_size == 2 ? 60.0f : 50.0f;
    const float leading = size * 1.16f;
    // A whole-cue ASS color outranks the yellow preference; a cue that mixes
    // colors keeps the default rather than guessing wrong (see plain_cue).
    const Color color = state.sub_fg >= 0 ? Color::rgb(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(state.sub_fg))
                                          : (state.sub_color == 1 ? Color::rgb(0xffe14d) : Color::rgb(0xffffff));
    const float lift = state.sub_raised ? 120.0f : 0.0f;
    // Subtitles rise out of the way of the controls, and above the bottom-right
    // cards (up-next at y 688; the skip prompt stacks above it at y 548) so
    // dialogue never slides under them.
    float raise = 150.0f * tween::smoothstep(controls_.value);
    float card_top = 1080.0f;
    if (!state.upnext_title.empty())
        card_top = 688.0f;
    if (!state.skip_prompt.empty() && controls_.value > 0.01f)
        card_top = state.upnext_title.empty() ? 688.0f : 548.0f; // mirror the card's own y
    if (card_top < 1080.0f)
        raise = std::max(raise, 960.0f - lift - (card_top - 28.0f) + size * 0.3f);
    std::vector<std::string> lines;
    for (std::size_t start = 0; start <= state.line.size();)
    {
        std::size_t end = state.line.find('\n', start);
        if (end == std::string::npos)
            end = state.line.size();
        wrap_subtitle_line(env_.fonts.semibold.font, state.line.substr(start, end - start), size,
                           theme::kScreenW - 220.0f, lines);
        start = end + 1;
    }
    if (lines.empty()) // over-wide all-whitespace cue: nothing to draw
        return;
    // ASS \an alignment: 7/8/9 pin to the top (signs), 1/4/7 left, 3/6/9 right.
    const int an = state.sub_an;
    const float sx = an == 1 || an == 4 || an == 7 ? 110.0f : an == 3 || an == 6 || an == 9 ? 1810.0f
                                                                                         : theme::kScreenW * 0.5f;
    const Align salign = an == 1 || an == 4 || an == 7 ? Align::left : an == 3 || an == 6 || an == 9 ? Align::right
                                                                                                   : Align::center;
    float baseline = an >= 7 ? 220.0f : 960.0f - lift - raise - leading * static_cast<float>(lines.size() - 1);
    if (state.sub_bg_opacity > 0)
    {
        // One merged background for the whole cue. Per-line boxes are taller
        // (1.52x the font size) than the line spacing (1.16x), so with two
        // lines the boxes overlapped and doubled the darkening between them.
        float wmax = 0.0f;
        for (const std::string &line : lines)
            wmax = std::max(wmax, env_.fonts.semibold.font->measure(line, size));
        const float px = size * 0.35f, pt = size * 0.22f, pb = size * 0.30f;
        const float last = baseline + leading * static_cast<float>(lines.size() - 1);
        const float bx = salign == Align::left ? sx - px : salign == Align::right ? sx - wmax - px : sx - wmax * 0.5f - px;
        list.rounded_rect({bx, baseline - size - pt, wmax + px * 2.0f, last + pb - (baseline - size - pt)},
                          size * 0.16f, Color::rgb(0x000000, state.sub_bg_opacity / 100.0f));
    }
    for (const std::string &line : lines)
    {
        draw_outlined_text(list, env_.fonts.semibold, line, sx, baseline, size, color, salign);
        baseline += leading;
    }
}

void PlayerUi::draw_upnext(hui::gfx::DrawList &fg) const
{
    if (state.upnext_title.empty())
        return;
    // Netflix-style card, bottom right: what is coming, the countdown, and the way out.
    // Fades and slides up on entry.
    const float t = upnext_.value;
    fg.push_opacity(t);
    const float cw = 560.0f, ch = 216.0f;
    const float x = theme::kScreenW - cw - 80.0f, y = 688.0f + 24.0f * (1.0f - t);
    draw_glass_panel(fg, env_, {x, y, cw, ch}, 20.0f, Color::rgb(0x000000, 0.72f));
    text(fg, env_.fonts.semibold, "Up next", x + 36.0f, y + 54.0f, 24.0f, theme::accent_soft);
    text(fg, env_.fonts.semibold, env_.fonts.semibold.font->fit(state.upnext_title, 30.0f, cw - 72.0f), x + 36.0f,
         y + 100.0f, 30.0f, theme::ink);
    if (state.upnext_remaining >= 0)
    {
        char buf[32];
        std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "Starting in %d",
                      std::max(1, static_cast<int>(std::ceil(state.upnext_remaining))));
        text(fg, env_.fonts.regular, buf, x + 36.0f, y + 146.0f, 26.0f, theme::ink_2);
    }
    // The fast way out: Cross jumps straight to the next episode.
    const float bw = 168.0f, bh = 44.0f, bx = x + 36.0f, by = y + 160.0f;
    draw_glass_panel(fg, env_, {bx, by, bw, bh}, bh * 0.5f, theme::glass_fill);
    hui::ui::draw_button(fg, env_.fonts, hui::ui::GlyphStyle::dark(), hui::ui::Button::cross, bx + 24.0f, by + bh * 0.5f,
                         30.0f);
    text(fg, env_.fonts.semibold, "Next", bx + 64.0f, by + 30.0f, 24.0f, theme::ink);
    text(fg, env_.fonts.regular, "Any other button cancels", bx + bw + 18.0f, by + 30.0f, 22.0f, theme::ink_3);
    fg.pop_opacity();
}

void PlayerUi::draw_binge(hui::gfx::DrawList &fg) const
{
    if (!state.binge_prompt)
        return;
    // Centered card: the binge guard is asking whether anyone is still there.
    // Fades and scales up on entry.
    const float t = binge_.value;
    fg.push_opacity(t);
    const float cw = 660.0f, ch = 300.0f;
    const float x = (theme::kScreenW - cw) * 0.5f, y = (theme::kScreenH - ch) * 0.5f;
    const float cx = x + cw * 0.5f;
    fg.push_transform(0.96f + 0.04f * t, cx, y + ch * 0.5f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
    draw_glass_panel(fg, env_, {x, y, cw, ch}, 24.0f, Color::rgb(0x000000, 0.85f));
    text(fg, env_.fonts.semibold, "Still watching?", cx, y + 92.0f, 44.0f, theme::ink, Align::center);
    const float bw = 240.0f, bh = 56.0f, bx = cx - bw * 0.5f, by = y + 140.0f;
    draw_glass_panel(fg, env_, {bx, by, bw, bh}, bh * 0.5f, Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.08f));
    hui::ui::draw_button(fg, env_.fonts, hui::ui::GlyphStyle::dark(), hui::ui::Button::cross, bx + 36.0f,
                         by + bh * 0.5f, 32.0f);
    text(fg, env_.fonts.semibold, "Continue", bx + 84.0f, by + 38.0f, 26.0f, theme::ink);
    char buf[32];
    std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "Stopping in %d",
                  std::max(1, static_cast<int>(std::ceil(state.binge_left))));
    text(fg, env_.fonts.regular, buf, cx, y + 258.0f, 24.0f, theme::ink_3, Align::center);
    fg.pop_transform();
    fg.pop_opacity();
}

void PlayerUi::draw_skip_prompt(hui::gfx::DrawList &fg) const
{
    if (state.skip_prompt.empty() || controls_.value <= 0.01f)
        return;
    // Bottom-right card, mirroring the up-next card. Stacked above it when
    // both are visible (an outro in the last 20 s of the episode).
    // Fades in on entry; the y glides instead of teleporting when up-next appears.
    const float t = skip_.value;
    fg.push_opacity(t);
    const float cw = 420.0f, ch = 120.0f;
    const float x = theme::kScreenW - cw - 80.0f;
    const float y = skip_y_.value;
    draw_glass_panel(fg, env_, {x, y, cw, ch}, 20.0f, Color::rgb(0x000000, 0.72f));
    hui::ui::draw_button(fg, env_.fonts, hui::ui::GlyphStyle::dark(), hui::ui::Button::triangle, x + 36.0f,
                         y + ch * 0.5f, 44.0f);
    text(fg, env_.fonts.semibold, state.skip_prompt, x + 100.0f, y + 50.0f, 30.0f, theme::ink);
    text(fg, env_.fonts.regular, "Skip", x + 100.0f, y + 86.0f, 22.0f, theme::ink_2);
    fg.pop_opacity();
}

void PlayerUi::draw_volume_osd(hui::gfx::DrawList &fg) const
{
    if (state.volume_osd < 0)
        return;
    // Top-center pill: speaker icon + bar + percent. Shows for 1.5 s after
    // a touchpad swipe changes the volume.
    const float pw = 520.0f, ph = 96.0f;
    const float x = (theme::kScreenW - pw) * 0.5f;
    const float y = 120.0f;
    draw_glass_panel(fg, env_, {x, y, pw, ph}, 24.0f, Color::rgb(0x000000, 0.72f));
    text(fg, env_.fonts.semibold, "Volume", x + 36.0f, y + 38.0f, 28.0f, theme::ink);
    // Bar: 280 px wide, 12 px tall, centered vertically.
    const float bx = x + 180.0f, bw = 280.0f, bh = 12.0f;
    const float by = y + (ph - bh) * 0.5f;
    fg.rounded_rect({bx, by, bw, bh}, 6.0f, Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.25f));
    const float fill = bw * (state.volume_osd / 100.0f);
    if (fill > 0.5f)
        fg.rounded_rect({bx, by, fill, bh}, 6.0f, Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.9f));
    char buf[16];
    std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%d%%", state.volume_osd);
    text(fg, env_.fonts.regular, buf, bx + bw + 16.0f, y + 38.0f, 28.0f, theme::ink);
}

void PlayerUi::draw_controls(hui::gfx::DrawList &bg, hui::gfx::DrawList &fg, float amount) const
{
    bg.push_opacity(amount);
    fg.push_opacity(amount);
    bg.gradient_rect({0, 0, theme::kScreenW, 240.0f}, 0, Color::rgb(0x000000, 0.72f), Color::rgb(0x000000, 0.0f));
    bg.gradient_rect({0, 640.0f, theme::kScreenW, 440.0f}, 0, Color::rgb(0x000000, 0.0f), Color::rgb(0x000000, 0.86f));

    // The stats-for-nerds panel sits top-right (x 1260-1880): keep a long
    // title from running under it.
    const float title_w = state.nerd_overlay ? 1100.0f : 1500.0f;
    text(bg, env_.fonts.semibold, env_.fonts.semibold.font->fit(state.title, 42.0f, title_w), 110.0f, 108.0f, 42.0f,
         theme::ink, Align::left, -0.4f);
    if (!state.subtitle.empty())
        text(bg, env_.fonts.regular, env_.fonts.regular.font->fit(state.subtitle, 28.0f, title_w), 110.0f, 152.0f,
             28.0f, theme::ink_2);

    // The seek bar.
    const float x = 110.0f, w = 1700.0f, y = 862.0f;
    const double duration = std::max(1.0, state.duration);
    const float played = static_cast<float>(std::clamp(state.position / duration, 0.0, 1.0));
    const float ready = static_cast<float>(std::clamp((state.position + state.buffered) / duration, 0.0, 1.0));
    bg.rounded_rect({x, y, w, 8.0f}, 4.0f, Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.62f));
    bg.rounded_rect({x, y, w * std::max(ready, played), 8.0f}, 4.0f, Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.80f));
    // Intro/outro bands: over the track and the buffered fill, under the played progress,
    // so they stay visible in buffered regions.
    for (const auto &sg : state.skip_segments)
    {
        const float x0 = x + w * static_cast<float>(std::clamp(sg.start / duration, 0.0, 1.0));
        const float x1 = x + w * static_cast<float>(std::clamp(sg.end / duration, 0.0, 1.0));
        if (x1 > x0 + 1.0f)
            bg.rounded_rect({x0, y, x1 - x0, 8.0f}, 4.0f, Color::rgb(0xffb347, 0.85f));
    }
    bg.glow({x, y, w * played, 8.0f}, 4.0f, 12.0f, theme::accent.with_alpha(0.55f));
    bg.rounded_rect({x, y, w * played, 8.0f}, 4.0f, theme::accent_soft);
    bg.circle(x + w * played, y + 4.0f, 14.0f, Color::rgb(0xffffff));
    // Seek-preview bubble: while a D-pad/L1/R1 seek is in flight, show the
    // target time above the bar at the target position. (A real thumbnail
    // would need a parallel decoder; the player decodes sequentially, so a
    // time label is the honest version.)
    if (state.seek_preview >= 0)
    {
        const float tx = x + w * static_cast<float>(std::clamp(state.seek_preview / duration, 0.0, 1.0));
        const std::string label = clock_text(state.seek_preview);
        const float tw = env_.fonts.semibold.font->measure(label, 26.0f) + 36.0f;
        const float bx = std::clamp(tx - tw / 2.0f, x, x + w - tw);
        bg.rounded_rect({bx, y - 58.0f, tw, 44.0f}, 22.0f, Color::rgb(0x000000, 0.72f));
        text(bg, env_.fonts.semibold, label, bx + tw / 2.0f, y - 48.0f, 26.0f, theme::ink, Align::center);
    }
    text(bg, env_.fonts.semibold, clock_text(state.position), x, y + 56.0f, 26.0f, theme::ink);
    text(bg, env_.fonts.regular, "-" + clock_text(state.duration - state.position), x + w, y + 56.0f, 26.0f, theme::ink_2,
         Align::right);

    // Tracks on the left, hints on the right. The hints are measured first and
    // the chips get whatever room is left. The row only fits if the hint
    // labels stay short: keep them terse, or the chip budget below goes
    // negative and the chips collapse (see the floor there).
    hui::ui::HintLayout layout;
    layout.size = 36.0f;
    layout.text_size = 24.0f;
    layout.cy = 1010.0f;
    layout.item_gap = 40.0f;
    layout.font = &env_.fonts.regular;
    // While the up-next card is up, Cross belongs to it ("Next"), not to play/pause.
    // With the track menu open Cross selects a track instead, so the hint must
    // not claim "Next" there.
    const bool upnext_next = !state.upnext_title.empty() && !state.menu;
    // In the Tracks menu the buttons do menu things: Cross selects a track,
    // Circle closes, L1/R1 nudge audio delay, L2/R2 nudge subtitle delay.
    const hui::ui::Hint menu_hints[4] = {{hui::ui::Button::cross, "Select"},
                                         {hui::ui::Button::circle, "Close"},
                                         {hui::ui::Button::l1, "Audio delay", hui::ui::Button::r1},
                                         {hui::ui::Button::l2, "Sub delay", hui::ui::Button::r2}};
    // One d-pad hint, not three: three labels made the row wider than the
    // screen and the chips slid under the hints (PS5 2026-10-13).
    const hui::ui::Hint hints[7] = {{hui::ui::Button::cross, upnext_next ? "Next" : (state.paused ? "Play" : "Pause")},
                                    {hui::ui::Button::dpad, "10 s · Tracks · Zoom"},
                                    {hui::ui::Button::l1, "1 min", hui::ui::Button::r1},
                                    {hui::ui::Button::l2, "Sub sync", hui::ui::Button::r2},
                                    {hui::ui::Button::right_stick, "Stats"},
                                    {hui::ui::Button::circle, "Stop"},
                                    {hui::ui::Button::triangle, "Skip"}};
    // The Skip hint only appears while its prompt is up (Triangle is the
    // audio-track cycle the rest of the time).
    const hui::ui::Hint *active_hints = state.menu ? menu_hints : hints;
    const int hint_count = state.menu ? 4 : (state.skip_prompt.empty() ? 6 : 7);
    const float hints_w = hui::ui::measure_hints(env_.fonts, active_hints, hint_count, layout);

    // Long track names share what room the hints leave: the shorter one
    // keeps its full name, the longer one gives way. The Tracks chip is
    // fixed-width: 12 + glyph(36) + 12 + text + 22 (see draw_chip), and the
    // audio/subs chips each carry the same 82px of chrome plus a 14px gap.
    // The floor below is 1px, not 200: a 200px floor on the *text* budget
    // ignores the ~364px of fixed chrome, so when the hints get wide the
    // chips blew past the hints' start and the glyphs drew over the chip
    // text (PS5 2026-10-13). With a 1px floor the chips collapse to empty
    // pills instead of ever overlapping; the hint labels above are kept
    // short so the budget stays positive in practice.
    const std::string audio = "Audio  " + state.audio, subs = "Subtitles  " + state.subs;
    const float tracks_w = 82.0f + env_.fonts.semibold.measure("Tracks", theme::kBody);
    const float chip_chrome = 12.0f + 36.0f + 12.0f + 22.0f; // draw_chip's fixed part
    const float budget =
        std::max(1.0f, x + w - hints_w - 28.0f - x - tracks_w - 2 * chip_chrome - 2 * 14.0f);
    const float na = env_.fonts.semibold.measure(audio.c_str(), theme::kBody);
    const float ns = env_.fonts.semibold.measure(subs.c_str(), theme::kBody);
    float ma = na, ms = ns;
    if (na + ns > budget)
    {
        if (na <= budget * 0.5f)
            ms = budget - na;
        else if (ns <= budget * 0.5f)
            ma = budget - ns;
        else
            ma = ms = budget * 0.5f;
    }
    float cx = x;
    // One glass pill behind the whole row: chips on the left, hints on the
    // right, grouped the way the theme groups its control cluster.
    draw_glass_panel(fg, env_, {x - 18.0f, 1010.0f - 32.0f, w + 36.0f, 64.0f}, 20.0f, theme::glass_fill);
    cx += draw_chip(fg, env_, hui::ui::Button::triangle, audio.c_str(), cx, 1010.0f, false, ma) + 14.0f;
    cx += draw_chip(fg, env_, hui::ui::Button::square, subs.c_str(), cx, 1010.0f, false, ms) + 14.0f;
    draw_chip(fg, env_, hui::ui::Button::options, "Tracks", cx, 1010.0f);
    hui::ui::draw_hints(fg, env_.fonts, hui::ui::GlyphStyle::dark(), active_hints, hint_count, x + w, true, layout);
    bg.pop_opacity();
    fg.pop_opacity();
}

void PlayerUi::draw_buffering(hui::gfx::DrawList &list, float amount) const
{
    list.push_opacity(amount);
    list.rounded_rect({0, 0, theme::kScreenW, theme::kScreenH}, 0, Color::rgb(0x000000, 0.38f));
    const float cx = theme::kScreenW * 0.5f, cy = 470.0f;
    // No ring: the loading progress lives in artwork, never a spinner ring.
    // A breathing dot marks the wait instead.
    const float breathe = 0.45f + 0.55f * (0.5f + 0.5f * std::sin(clock_ * 3.0f));
    list.circle(cx, cy, 20.0f, theme::accent_soft.with_alpha(0.85f * breathe));
    char label[48];
    std::snprintf(label, sizeof(label), "Buffering  %d%%", state.buffering);
    text(list, env_.fonts.semibold, label, cx, cy + 128.0f, 36.0f, theme::ink, Align::center);
    if (!state.net.empty())
        text(list, env_.fonts.regular, state.net, cx, cy + 172.0f, 26.0f, theme::ink_2, Align::center);
    list.pop_opacity();
}

void PlayerUi::draw_nerd(hui::gfx::DrawList &fg) const
{
    if (!state.nerd_overlay || state.nerd_lines.empty())
        return;
    // Compact panel, top-right, clear of the title block.
    const float w = 620.0f;
    const float h = 56.0f + 30.0f * static_cast<float>(state.nerd_lines.size());
    const float x = theme::kScreenW - w - 40.0f, y = 40.0f;
    draw_glass_panel(fg, env_, {x, y, w, h}, 12.0f, Color::rgb(0x000000, 0.72f));
    text(fg, env_.fonts.semibold, "Stats", x + 20.0f, y + 36.0f, 24.0f, theme::ink);
    float yy = y + 70.0f;
    for (const auto &line : state.nerd_lines)
    {
        text(fg, env_.fonts.regular, env_.fonts.regular.font->fit(line, 22.0f, w - 40.0f), x + 20.0f, yy, 22.0f,
             theme::ink_2);
        yy += 30.0f;
    }
}

void PlayerUi::draw_menu(hui::gfx::DrawList &bg, hui::gfx::DrawList &fg, float amount) const
{
    bg.push_opacity(amount);
    fg.push_opacity(amount);
    bg.rounded_rect({0, 0, theme::kScreenW, theme::kScreenH}, 0, Color::rgb(0x080808, 0.55f));
    const Rect panel{260.0f, 150.0f, 1400.0f, 780.0f};
    fg.push_transform(0.97f + 0.03f * amount, panel.cx(), panel.cy(), 0.0f, 0.0f);
    fg.shadow({panel.x + 10, panel.y + 30, panel.w - 20, panel.h - 20}, 38, 60, Color::rgb(0x000000, 0.55f));
    draw_glass_panel(fg, env_, panel, 38.0f, Color::rgb(0x101012, 0.86f));
    struct Column
    {
        const char *title;
        const std::vector<std::string> *tracks;
        int active;
        float x;
        float w;
    };
    // Four columns share the panel: audio + subtitles keep room for names,
    // speed and skip are narrow action columns.
    const Column columns[4] = {{"Audio", &state.audio_tracks, state.audio_active, panel.x + 50.0f, 400.0f},
                               {"Subtitles", &state.subtitle_tracks, state.subtitle_active, panel.x + 480.0f, 400.0f},
                               {"Speed", &state.speed_options, state.speed_active, panel.x + 910.0f, 220.0f},
                               {"Skip", &state.skip_options, state.skip_active, panel.x + 1160.0f, 240.0f}};
    for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
    {
        const bool focused = state.menu_column == c;
        const float x = columns[c].x;
        fg.push_opacity(focused ? 1.0f : 0.55f);
        text(fg, env_.fonts.semibold, columns[c].title, x, panel.y + 88.0f, 34.0f, theme::ink);
        // Long lists scroll: the window follows the cursor, or the ticked track.
        const int total = static_cast<int>(columns[c].tracks->size());
        const int anchor = focused ? state.menu_row : columns[c].active;
        const int first = std::max(0, std::min(anchor - 3, total - 7));
        for (int k = 0; k < 7 && first + k < total; ++k)
        {
            const int i = first + k;
            const Rect r{x, panel.y + 124.0f + static_cast<float>(k) * 76.0f, columns[c].w, 66.0f};
            const bool selected = focused && state.menu_row == i;
            if (selected)
            {
                fg.glow(r, 24.0f, 22.0f, theme::accent.with_alpha(0.32f));
                fg.bordered_rect(r, 24.0f, theme::surface_hi, 3.0f, Color::rgb(0xffffff));
            }
            else
            {
                fg.rounded_rect(r, 24.0f, Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.05f));
            }
            const bool active = i == columns[c].active;
            if (active)
            {
                fg.line(r.x + 26.0f, r.cy() + 1.0f, r.x + 34.0f, r.cy() + 10.0f, 3.6f, theme::accent_soft);
                fg.line(r.x + 34.0f, r.cy() + 10.0f, r.x + 50.0f, r.cy() - 9.0f, 3.6f, theme::accent_soft);
            }
            const hui::ui::FontRef &face = active ? env_.fonts.semibold : env_.fonts.regular;
            text(fg, face, face.font->fit((*columns[c].tracks)[i], 27.0f, r.w - 110.0f), r.x + 70.0f, r.cy() + 9.0f, 27.0f,
                 active || selected ? theme::ink : theme::ink_2);
        }
        fg.pop_opacity();
    }
    text(fg, env_.fonts.regular, "Subtitle delay  " + (state.subtitle_delay.empty() ? std::string("0.00 s") : state.subtitle_delay) + "   (L2 / R2)",
         panel.x + 710.0f, panel.y + panel.h - 48.0f, 24.0f, theme::ink_3);
    text(fg, env_.fonts.regular, "Audio delay  " + (state.audio_delay.empty() ? std::string("0.00 s") : state.audio_delay) + "   (L1 / R1)",
         panel.x + 710.0f, panel.y + panel.h - 80.0f, 24.0f, theme::ink_3);
    draw_chip(fg, env_, hui::ui::Button::circle, "Close", panel.x + panel.w - 200.0f, panel.y + panel.h - 56.0f);
    fg.pop_transform();
    fg.pop_opacity();
    bg.pop_opacity();
}

void PlayerUi::draw(hui::gfx::DrawList &bg, hui::gfx::DrawList &fg) const
{
    if (controls_.value > 0.01f)
        draw_controls(bg, fg, controls_.value);
    draw_upnext(fg);
    draw_binge(fg);
    draw_skip_prompt(fg);
    draw_volume_osd(fg);
    draw_nerd(fg);
    if (buffering_.value < 0.5f && menu_.value < 0.5f)
        draw_subtitle(bg);
    if (paused_.value > 0.01f && menu_.value < 0.5f)
    {
        bg.push_opacity(paused_.value * (1.0f - menu_.value));
        const float cx = theme::kScreenW * 0.5f, cy = 470.0f;
        bg.shadow({cx - 90, cy - 70, 180, 180}, 90, 40, Color::rgb(0x000000, 0.4f));
        bg.circle(cx, cy, 90.0f, Color::rgb(0x000000, 0.55f));
        bg.ring(cx, cy, 89.0f, 2.0f, Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.22f));
        bg.rounded_rect({cx - 34.0f, cy - 36.0f, 20.0f, 72.0f}, 7.0f, Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.96f));
        bg.rounded_rect({cx + 14.0f, cy - 36.0f, 20.0f, 72.0f}, 7.0f, Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.96f));
        bg.pop_opacity();
    }
    if (buffering_.value > 0.01f)
        draw_buffering(bg, buffering_.value);
    if (menu_.value > 0.01f)
        draw_menu(bg, fg, menu_.value);
}

} // namespace sx


===== FILE: ui/player.hpp =====
// What is drawn over the video: the controls, the paused and buffering
// states, subtitles and the track menu. The picture itself is the player's.

#pragma once

#include "chrome.hpp"
#include "content.hpp"

namespace sx
{

class PlayerUi
{
  public:
    explicit PlayerUi(const Env &env) : env_(env)
    {
        skip_y_.snap(688.0f);
    }

    PlayerState state; // the app keeps this up to date

    void snap();       // jump every animation to where the state wants it
    void update(float dt);
    void draw(hui::gfx::DrawList &bg, hui::gfx::DrawList &fg) const;

  private:
    void draw_controls(hui::gfx::DrawList &bg, hui::gfx::DrawList &fg, float amount) const;
    void draw_menu(hui::gfx::DrawList &bg, hui::gfx::DrawList &fg, float amount) const;
    void draw_buffering(hui::gfx::DrawList &list, float amount) const;
    void draw_subtitle(hui::gfx::DrawList &list) const;
    void draw_upnext(hui::gfx::DrawList &list) const;
    void draw_binge(hui::gfx::DrawList &list) const;
    void draw_skip_prompt(hui::gfx::DrawList &list) const;
    void draw_volume_osd(hui::gfx::DrawList &list) const;
    void draw_nerd(hui::gfx::DrawList &list) const;

    const Env &env_;
    hui::tween::Spring controls_;
    hui::tween::Spring menu_;
    hui::tween::Spring paused_;
    hui::tween::Spring buffering_;
    hui::tween::Spring upnext_;
    hui::tween::Spring binge_;
    hui::tween::Spring skip_;
    hui::tween::Spring skip_y_;
    float clock_ = 0.0f;
};

// Text with a dark outline, for subtitles over any picture.
void draw_outlined_text(hui::gfx::DrawList &list, const hui::ui::FontRef &font, const std::string &value, float x,
                        float baseline, float size, hui::gfx::Color colour, hui::gfx::Align align);

} // namespace sx


===== FILE: ui/settings.cpp =====
#include "settings.hpp"

#include <algorithm>

namespace sx
{

using hui::Direction;
using hui::gfx::Align;
using hui::gfx::Color;
using hui::gfx::Rect;
using hui::ui::text;
namespace tween = hui::tween;

namespace
{

constexpr float kListX = theme::kLeft;
constexpr float kListW = 1040.0f;
constexpr float kRowH = 90.0f;
constexpr float kPitch = 100.0f;
constexpr float kTop = 196.0f;
constexpr Rect kHelp{1290.0f, 196.0f, 570.0f, 640.0f};

} // namespace

Rect Settings::row_rect(int index, bool final_position) const
{
    const float scroll = final_position ? scroll_.position.target : scroll_.offset();
    return {kListX, kTop + static_cast<float>(index) * kPitch - scroll, kListW, kRowH};
}

bool Settings::is_header(int index) const
{
    return content_ && index >= 0 && index < static_cast<int>(content_->rows.size()) &&
           content_->rows[static_cast<std::size_t>(index)].kind == SettingRow::Kind::header;
}

// Rows with an empty label are hidden (e.g. "Sharpening strength" when the
// upscaler doesn't use it): never focused, never drawn.
bool Settings::is_selectable(int index) const
{
    return content_ && index >= 0 && index < static_cast<int>(content_->rows.size()) && !is_header(index) &&
           !content_->rows[static_cast<std::size_t>(index)].label.empty();
}

int Settings::first_selectable() const
{
    if (!content_)
        return 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(content_->rows.size()); ++i)
        if (is_selectable(i))
            return i;
    return 0;
}

void Settings::set_content(const SettingsContent *content)
{
    content_ = content;
    index_ = first_selectable();
    shown_ = -1;
    switches_.assign(content ? content->rows.size() : 0, {});
    if (content)
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < content->rows.size(); ++i)
            switches_[i].snap(content->rows[i].on ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
    scroll_.position.snap(0.0f);
    ring_.snap(row_rect(index_, true));
    focus_on_.snap(1.0f);
}

void Settings::refresh()
{
    if (!content_)
        return;
    const std::size_t rows = content_->rows.size();
    const std::size_t before = switches_.size();
    switches_.resize(rows);
    for (std::size_t i = before; i < rows; ++i)
        switches_[i].snap(content_->rows[i].on ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
    index_ = std::min(index_, std::max(0, static_cast<int>(rows) - 1));
    if (!is_selectable(index_))
        index_ = first_selectable();
}

void Settings::update(const hui::InputFrame &input, float dt)
{
    tick(dt);
    if (!content_ || content_->rows.empty())
        return;
    const int count = static_cast<int>(content_->rows.size());
    if (!rail_input(input))
    {
        if (input.nav == Direction::up)
        {
            // Section headers are labels, hidden rows are skipped: skip over both.
            for (int i = index_ - 1; i >= 0; --i)
                if (is_selectable(i))
                {
                    index_ = i;
                    break;
                }
        }
        else if (input.nav == Direction::down)
        {
            for (int i = index_ + 1; i < count; ++i)
                if (is_selectable(i))
                {
                    index_ = i;
                    break;
                }
        }
        else if (input.nav == Direction::left)
            to_rail();
    }
    const float top = static_cast<float>(index_) * kPitch;
    scroll_.reveal(top, top + kRowH, 1080.0f - kTop - 60.0f, 14.0f);
    scroll_.update(dt, 18.0f);
    ring_.target(row_rect(index_, true));
    ring_.update(dt, 22.0f);
    focus_on_.target = rail_.focused() ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
    focus_on_.update(dt, 16.0f);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < switches_.size(); ++i)
    {
        switches_[i].target = content_->rows[i].on ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
        switches_[i].update(dt, 22.0f);
    }
    if (index_ != shown_)
    {
        shown_ = index_;
        swap_.start(0.28f);
    }
    // A row hidden since the last refresh (empty label) must not keep the cursor.
    if (!is_selectable(index_))
        index_ = first_selectable();
    swap_.update(dt);
}

void Settings::draw_help(hui::gfx::DrawList &fg, const SettingRow &row) const
{
    const float in = tween::smoothstep(swap_.progress());
    const float panel_in = tween::stagger(age_, 2, 0.05f, 0.5f);
    fg.push_opacity(panel_in);
    draw_glass_panel(fg, env_, kHelp, 36.0f, Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.05f));
    const float x = kHelp.x + 38.0f;
    const float w = kHelp.w - 76.0f;
    fg.push_opacity(in);
    const float rise = 8.0f * (1.0f - in);
    const hui::ui::FontRef &display = env_.fonts.display;
    std::vector<std::string> lines = display.font->wrap(row.label, 38.0f, w);
    if (lines.size() > 2)
        lines.resize(2);
    float baseline = kHelp.y + 86.0f + rise;
    for (const std::string &line : lines)
    {
        text(fg, display, line, x, baseline, 38.0f, theme::ink, Align::left, -0.8f);
        baseline += 46.0f;
    }
    // The current value, in the page's violet.
    std::string value = row.value;
    if (row.kind == SettingRow::Kind::toggle)
        value = row.on ? "On" : "Off";
    text(fg, env_.fonts.semibold, env_.fonts.semibold.font->fit(value, 28.0f, w), x, baseline + 6.0f, 28.0f,
         theme::accent_soft);
    hui::ui::paragraph(fg, env_.fonts.regular, row.help, x, baseline + 64.0f, 24.0f, w, 36.0f, theme::ink_2, 9);
    const char *verb = row.kind == SettingRow::Kind::toggle ? "Switch"
                       : row.kind == SettingRow::Kind::action  ? "Open"
                       : row.kind == SettingRow::Kind::slider ? "Adjust"
                                                              : "Change";
    draw_chip(fg, env_, hui::ui::Button::cross, verb, x, kHelp.y + kHelp.h - 62.0f, true);
    fg.pop_opacity();
    fg.pop_opacity();
}

void Settings::draw(hui::gfx::DrawList &bg, hui::gfx::DrawList &fg, hui::gfx::BackdropSpec &backdrop) const
{
    apply_page_backdrop(backdrop, clock_);
    if (!content_)
        return;
    const float in = tween::stagger(age_, 0, 0.0f, 0.45f);
    bg.push_opacity(in);
    text(bg, env_.fonts.semibold, "Settings", theme::kLeft, 158.0f, theme::kHeading, theme::ink_2);
    bg.pop_opacity();

    bg.push_clip({theme::kLeft - 44.0f, 188.0f, 1130.0f, 1080.0f - 188.0f});
    fg.push_clip({theme::kLeft - 44.0f, 188.0f, 1130.0f, 1080.0f - 188.0f});
    const Rect ring = ring_.value();
    if (focus_on_.value > 0.01f)
    {
        fg.push_opacity(focus_on_.value);
        fg.glow(ring, 24.0f, 26.0f, theme::accent.with_alpha(0.34f));
        fg.pop_opacity();
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(content_->rows.size()); ++i)
    {
        const Rect r = row_rect(i, false);
        if (r.y > 1080.0f || r.y + r.h < 180.0f)
            continue;
        const SettingRow &row = content_->rows[i];
        if (row.label.empty() && row.kind != SettingRow::Kind::header)
            continue; // hidden row (e.g. sharpening when upscaler != FSR1+Sharpening)
        const bool focused = i == index_ && !rail_.focused();
        const float enter = tween::stagger(age_, i, 0.04f, 0.4f);
        // The focused row is a glass panel: it and its contents draw in the
        // foreground layer so they sample the blur; the rest stays put.
        hui::gfx::DrawList &rl = (focused && row.kind != SettingRow::Kind::header) ? fg : bg;
        rl.push_opacity(enter);
        if (row.kind == SettingRow::Kind::header)
        {
            // A section label: dimmer, no card, no chevron, never focused.
            text(rl, env_.fonts.semibold, row.label, r.x + 34.0f, r.cy() + 8.0f, 24.0f, theme::ink_3);
        }
        else
        {
            rl.push_transform(1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 14.0f * (1.0f - enter));
            if (focused)
                draw_glass_panel(rl, env_, r, 24.0f, theme::glass_fill);
            else
                rl.rounded_rect(r, 24.0f, theme::surface);
            text(rl, env_.fonts.semibold, row.label, r.x + 34.0f, r.cy() + 10.0f, 28.0f, focused ? theme::ink : theme::ink_2);
            const float right = r.x + r.w - 34.0f;
            if (row.kind == SettingRow::Kind::toggle)
            {
                draw_switch(rl, right - 34.0f, r.cy(), switches_[i].value);
            }
            else if (row.kind == SettingRow::Kind::slider)
            {
                // A horizontal bar showing the fill, then the % value. No
                // chevron: Left/Right adjusts it in place.
                const float pct = std::clamp(row.slider_pct, 0, 100) / 100.0f;
                const float bw = 220.0f, bh = 8.0f;
                const float bx = right - 80.0f - bw;
                const float by = r.cy() - bh * 0.5f;
                rl.rounded_rect({bx, by, bw, bh}, bh * 0.5f, Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.14f));
                if (pct > 0.0f)
                    rl.rounded_rect({bx, by, bw * pct, bh}, bh * 0.5f,
                                    focused ? theme::accent : theme::accent_soft);
                text(rl, env_.fonts.regular, row.value, right, r.cy() + 9.0f, 26.0f,
                     focused ? theme::accent_soft : theme::ink_3, Align::right);
            }
            else
            {
                draw_chevron(rl, right - 8.0f, r.cy(), 16.0f, 1, focused ? theme::ink : theme::ink_3);
                text(rl, env_.fonts.regular, env_.fonts.regular.font->fit(row.value, 26.0f, 420.0f), right - 40.0f, r.cy() + 9.0f,
                     26.0f, focused ? theme::accent_soft : theme::ink_3, Align::right);
            }
            rl.pop_transform();
        }
        rl.pop_opacity();
    }
    if (focus_on_.value > 0.01f)
    {
        fg.push_opacity(focus_on_.value);
        fg.bordered_rect(ring, 24.0f, Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.0f), 4.0f, Color::rgb(0xffffff));
        fg.pop_opacity();
    }
    bg.pop_clip();
    fg.pop_clip();

    if (index_ >= 0 && index_ < static_cast<int>(content_->rows.size()))
        draw_help(fg, content_->rows[index_]);

    fade_down(bg, theme::kRailWidth, 0.0f, theme::kScreenW, 118.0f, 0.62f);
    fade_up(bg, theme::kRailWidth, 1000.0f, theme::kScreenW, 80.0f, 0.94f);
    draw_topbar(bg, fg, env_, false, age_);
    rail_.draw(fg, env_, age_);
}

} // namespace sx


===== FILE: ui/settings.hpp =====
// Settings: a list of choices and switches, and beside it what the focused one does.

#pragma once

#include "cards.hpp"
#include "page.hpp"

#include <vector>

namespace sx
{

class Settings : public PageBase
{
  public:
    explicit Settings(const Env &env) : PageBase(env, Page::settings)
    {
    }

    void set_content(const SettingsContent *content);
    void refresh(); // a value changed in place: the cursor stays
    void update(const hui::InputFrame &input, float dt);
    void draw(hui::gfx::DrawList &bg, hui::gfx::DrawList &fg, hui::gfx::BackdropSpec &backdrop) const;
    // The row under the cursor, or -1 while the rail has the focus.
    int cursor_row() const
    {
        return rail_.focused() ? -1 : index_;
    }
    // True when the cursor sits on a slider row (Left/Right adjusts it).
    bool cursor_on_slider() const
    {
        const int r = cursor_row();
        return r >= 0 && content_ && static_cast<std::size_t>(r) < content_->rows.size() &&
               content_->rows[static_cast<std::size_t>(r)].kind == SettingRow::Kind::slider;
    }

  private:
    hui::gfx::Rect row_rect(int index, bool final_position) const;
    void draw_help(hui::gfx::DrawList &list, const SettingRow &row) const;
    bool is_header(int index) const; // section labels: never focused or activated
    bool is_selectable(int index) const; // headers and hidden (empty-label) rows are skipped
    int first_selectable() const;

    const SettingsContent *content_ = nullptr;
    int index_ = 0;
    hui::ui::Scroller scroll_;
    hui::ui::SpringRect ring_;
    hui::tween::Spring focus_on_;
    std::vector<hui::tween::Spring> switches_; // 0..1 per row, so switches slide
    int shown_ = -1;
    hui::tween::Timer swap_;
};

} // namespace sx


===== FILE: ui/shell.cpp =====
#include "shell.hpp"

#include <algorithm>

namespace sx
{

using hui::Action;
using hui::Direction;

namespace
{
constexpr int kPageCount = 5;
}

Shell::Shell(const Env &env, Session &session)
    : env_(env), s_(session), board_(env), search_board_(env), discover_(env), library_(env), addons_(env), settings_(env),
      detail_(env), player_ui_(env)
{
    board_.set_content(&s_.board);
    search_board_.set_search(&s_.search_query);
    search_board_.set_content(&s_.search);
    discover_.set_content(&s_.discover);
    library_.set_content(&s_.library);
    addons_.set_content(&s_.addons);
    settings_.set_content(&s_.settings);
    go_page(0);
}

Session::Cursor Shell::cursor() const
{
    Session::Cursor c;
    c.board_row = board_.cursor_row();
    c.board_col = board_.cursor_col();
    c.discover = discover_.cursor_index();
    c.library = library_.cursor_index();
    c.episode = detail_.episode_index();
    c.search_row = search_board_.cursor_row();
    c.search_col = search_board_.cursor_col();
    return c;
}

void Shell::go_page(int page)
{
    page_ = std::clamp(page, 0, kPageCount - 1);
    screen_ = Screen::page;
    switch (page_)
    {
    case 0:
        board_.enter();
        break;
    case 1:
        discover_.enter();
        break;
    case 2:
        library_.enter();
        break;
    case 3:
        addons_.enter();
        break;
    default:
        settings_.enter();
        break;
    }
    s_.page_opened(static_cast<Page>(page_));
}

void Shell::open_title(const Title &title, const std::string &streams_video)
{
    came_from_ = screen_;
    s_.open_title(title, streams_video);
    detail_.set_content(&s_.detail);
    detail_.enter();
    screen_ = Screen::detail;
}

// Back from a title's page: to the page or the search results it was opened from.
void Shell::leave_title()
{
    s_.close_detail();
    if (came_from_ == Screen::search && s_.search_open)
    {
        screen_ = Screen::search;
        search_board_.enter();
    }
    else
    {
        go_page(page_);
    }
}

// What the session changed since the last frame, passed on to the screens that show it.
void Shell::apply_changes()
{
    const unsigned c = s_.take_changed();
    if (c & Session::kBoard)
        board_.refresh();
    if (c & Session::kDiscoverNew)
        discover_.set_content(&s_.discover);
    else if (c & Session::kDiscover)
        discover_.refresh();
    if (c & Session::kLibraryNew)
        library_.set_content(&s_.library);
    else if (c & Session::kLibrary)
        library_.refresh();
    if (c & Session::kAddons)
        addons_.refresh();
    if (c & Session::kSettings)
        settings_.refresh();
    if (c & Session::kDetail)
        detail_.refresh();
    if (c & Session::kSearchNew)
    {
        search_board_.set_content(&s_.search);
        search_board_.enter();
        if (s_.search_open)
            screen_ = Screen::search;
        else if (screen_ == Screen::search)
            screen_ = Screen::page;
    }
    else if (c & Session::kSearch)
    {
        search_board_.refresh();
    }
    Title requested;
    std::string requested_streams;
    if (s_.take_open(&requested, &requested_streams))
        open_title(requested, requested_streams);
    Page page;
    if (s_.take_goto(&page))
    {
        sound_.play(hui::audio::Cue::tab);
        go_page(static_cast<int>(page));
    }
}

void Shell::route_detail(const hui::InputFrame &input, float dt)
{
    if (input.pressed != 0 || input.nav != Direction::none)
        s_.cancel_autoplay(); // any button cancels playing the next episode
    const bool streams = detail_.mode() == Detail::Mode::streams;
    const bool series = s_.detail.series;
    // Back leaves the page, except from a series' streams, where it goes back to the episodes.
    const bool leave = input.is_pressed(Action::back) && !(series && streams);
    if (streams)
    {
        if (input.is_pressed(Action::confirm))
            s_.detail_play(detail_.stream_index(), false);
        else if (input.is_pressed(Action::west))
            s_.detail_play(detail_.stream_index(), true);
    }
    else
    {
        if (input.is_pressed(Action::page_prev))
            s_.detail_change_season(-1);
        else if (input.is_pressed(Action::page_next))
            s_.detail_change_season(1);
        else if (input.is_pressed(Action::north))
            s_.detail_toggle_watched(detail_.episode_index());
        else if (input.is_pressed(Action::confirm) && !s_.detail.episodes.empty())
            s_.detail_pick_episode(detail_.episode_index());
    }
    if (input.is_pressed(Action::menu))
        s_.detail_more_actions();
    detail_.update(input, dt);
    if (leave)
        leave_title();
}

void Shell::route_search(const hui::InputFrame &input, float dt)
{
    search_board_.update(input, dt);
    const bool on_rail = search_board_.rail_focused();
    if (input.is_pressed(Action::back))
    {
        s_.close_search();
        go_page(page_);
        return;
    }
    if (input.is_pressed(Action::north) || input.is_pressed(Action::menu))
    {
        s_.begin_search();
        return;
    }
    if (!input.is_pressed(Action::confirm))
        return;
    if (on_rail)
    {
        const int cursor = search_board_.rail_cursor();
        s_.close_search();
        sound_.play(hui::audio::Cue::tab);
        go_page(cursor);
    }
    else if (const Title *under = search_board_.focused_title())
    {
        open_title(*under);
    }
}

void Shell::route_page(const hui::InputFrame &input, float dt)
{
    const bool confirm = input.is_pressed(Action::confirm);
    bool on_rail = false;
    int cursor = 0;
    const Title *under = nullptr;
    switch (page_)
    {
    case 0:
        board_.update(input, dt);
        on_rail = board_.rail_focused();
        cursor = board_.rail_cursor();
        under = board_.focused_title();
        if (!on_rail && input.is_pressed(Action::west))
            s_.board_square(board_.cursor_row(), board_.cursor_col());
        // Options on a Continue Watching card: remove it (after asking). Elsewhere it is search.
        if (!on_rail && input.is_pressed(Action::menu) && s_.board_options(board_.cursor_row(), board_.cursor_col()))
            return;
        break;
    case 1:
        discover_.update(input, dt);
        on_rail = discover_.rail_focused();
        cursor = discover_.rail_cursor();
        under = discover_.focused_title();
        if (confirm && discover_.filter_cursor() >= 0)
        {
            s_.filter_activate(Page::discover, discover_.filter_cursor());
            return;
        }
        // Infinite scroll: fetch the next page when the cursor reaches the
        // last row. discover_load_more() no-ops while loading or at the end.
        if (discover_.at_end())
            s_.discover_load_more();
        break;
    case 2:
        library_.update(input, dt);
        on_rail = library_.rail_focused();
        cursor = library_.rail_cursor();
        under = library_.focused_title();
        if (confirm && library_.filter_cursor() >= 0)
        {
            s_.filter_activate(Page::library, library_.filter_cursor());
            return;
        }
        break;
    case 3:
        addons_.update(input, dt);
        on_rail = addons_.rail_focused();
        cursor = addons_.rail_cursor();
        if (!on_rail && confirm)
            s_.addon_activate(addons_.cursor_index());
        if (!on_rail && input.is_pressed(Action::west))
            s_.addons_reload();
        break;
    default:
    {
        // Slider rows: Left/Right adjusts the value in place instead of
        // moving focus (Left would otherwise jump to the rail).
        hui::InputFrame in = input;
        if (settings_.cursor_on_slider() &&
            (in.nav == Direction::left || in.nav == Direction::right))
        {
            s_.settings_nudge_slider(in.nav == Direction::right ? 1 : -1);
            in.nav = Direction::none;
        }
        settings_.update(in, dt);
        on_rail = settings_.rail_focused();
        cursor = settings_.rail_cursor();
        if (confirm && settings_.cursor_row() >= 0)
        {
            s_.settings_activate(settings_.cursor_row());
            return;
        }
        break;
    }
    }
    // Circle from any page but the Board goes to the Board (and from the Board it does nothing: the end of
    // the way back).
    if (page_ != 0 && input.is_pressed(Action::back))
    {
        go_page(0);
        return;
    }
    // Triangle or Options: search, from every page.
    if (!on_rail && (input.is_pressed(Action::north) || input.is_pressed(Action::menu)))
    {
        s_.begin_search();
        return;
    }
    if (!confirm)
        return;
    if (on_rail)
    {
        if (cursor != page_)
        {
            sound_.play(hui::audio::Cue::tab);
            go_page(cursor);
        }
    }
    else if (under)
    {
        // Glass Phase 1: the hero's buttons. Watch Now does what confirming
        // the focused card does; More Info opens the title's page.
        const int hero_btn = page_ == 0 ? board_.hero_button() : -1;
        if (hero_btn == 1)
            open_title(*under);
        // A card of Continue Watching asks what to do (or goes to the next episode's streams); any other opens.
        else if (page_ == 0 && s_.board_is_continue(board_.cursor_row()))
            s_.continue_press(board_.cursor_row(), board_.cursor_col());
        else
            open_title(*under);
    }
}

unsigned Shell::view_key() const
{
    return static_cast<unsigned>(screen_) * 64u + static_cast<unsigned>(page_) + (s_.dropdown_open ? 1000u : 0u);
}

unsigned Shell::focus_signature()
{
    const Session::Cursor c = cursor();
    unsigned h = 17u;
    const auto mix = [&h](int v) { h = h * 131u + static_cast<unsigned>(v + 7); };
    mix(c.board_row);
    mix(c.board_col);
    mix(c.discover);
    mix(c.library);
    mix(c.episode);
    mix(c.search_row);
    mix(c.search_col);
    mix(board_.rail_focused());
    mix(board_.rail_cursor());
    mix(discover_.rail_focused());
    mix(discover_.rail_cursor());
    mix(discover_.filter_cursor());
    mix(library_.rail_focused());
    mix(library_.rail_cursor());
    mix(library_.filter_cursor());
    mix(addons_.rail_focused());
    mix(addons_.rail_cursor());
    mix(addons_.cursor_index());
    mix(settings_.rail_focused());
    mix(settings_.rail_cursor());
    mix(settings_.cursor_row());
    mix(search_board_.rail_focused());
    mix(search_board_.rail_cursor());
    mix(detail_.stream_index());
    mix(detail_.episode_index());
    mix(s_.dropdown_open ? s_.dropdown.selected : -1);
    return h;
}
void Shell::update(const hui::InputFrame &input, float dt)
{
    clock_ += dt;
    // What arrived since the last frame, before any screen looks at its content.
    apply_changes();
    // The sounds: what the session wants played, and the cursor's move before and after the buttons.
    sound_.set_enabled(s_.ui_sounds());
    for (const hui::audio::Cue cue : s_.take_cues())
        sound_.play(cue);
    const bool sounds_ok = !s_.watching && !s_.sign_in_open && typing_hold_ <= 0.0f && boot_out_ >= 0.35f;
    const unsigned sig_before = sounds_ok ? focus_signature() : 0u;
    const unsigned view_before = view_key();
    if (sounds_ok && input.is_pressed(Action::back) && (s_.dropdown_open || screen_ != Screen::page || page_ != 0))
        sound_.play(hui::audio::Cue::back);
    const hui::InputFrame idle = [] {
        hui::InputFrame f;
        f.connected = true;
        return f;
    }();

    if (s_.dropdown_open)
    {
        DropdownState &d = s_.dropdown;
        const int count = static_cast<int>(d.options.size());
        if (input.nav == Direction::up)
            d.selected = std::max(0, d.selected - 1);
        else if (input.nav == Direction::down)
            d.selected = std::min(std::max(0, count - 1), d.selected + 1);
        if (input.is_pressed(Action::confirm))
            s_.dropdown_confirm();
        else if (input.is_pressed(Action::back))
            s_.dropdown_cancel();
    }
    else if (s_.sign_in_open)
    {
        if (input.is_pressed(Action::back))
            s_.sign_in_cancel();
    }
    // The keyboard dialog has the controller; its last button presses must not reach the screens.
    typing_hold_ = s_.typing() ? 0.35f : std::max(0.0f, typing_hold_ - dt);
    // The pop-ups hold the controller while they are up; the screens underneath keep moving.
    // The first moments: the mark is up until the first screen has its catalogs and posters; the
    // screens underneath are idle, and start their entrance when it lifts.
    if (boot_out_ < kBootOut)
        boot_t_ += dt;
    if (!s_.boot_ready())
    {
        boot_out_ = -1.0f;
    }
    else if (boot_out_ < 0.0f)
    {
        boot_out_ = 0.0f;
        board_.enter();
        sound_.play(hui::audio::Cue::welcome);
    }
    else
    {
        boot_out_ += dt;
    }
    const bool booting = boot_out_ < 0.35f;
    const bool held = s_.dropdown_open || s_.sign_in_open || dropdown_t_.value > 0.3f || typing_hold_ > 0.0f || booting;
    const hui::InputFrame &in = held ? idle : input;

    if (s_.watching)
    {
        if (!was_watching_)
            watch_t_ = 0.0f;
        watch_t_ += dt;
        s_.watch_input(in);
        player_ui_.state = s_.playing;
        player_ui_.update(dt);
    }
    else if (screen_ == Screen::detail)
    {
        route_detail(in, dt);
    }
    else if (screen_ == Screen::search)
    {
        route_search(in, dt);
    }
    else
    {
        route_page(in, dt);
    }
    if (was_watching_ && !s_.watching)
        sound_.play_when_back(hui::audio::Cue::resume);
    if (sounds_ok && input.nav != Direction::none && view_key() == view_before)
    {
        // A direction that moved the focus makes a soft sound; one that could not (the end of a list) a
        // short one that says so. Held directions stay silent at the end of a list: the initial press
        // already said it, and repeats would buzz several times a second.
        if (focus_signature() != sig_before)
            sound_.play(hui::audio::Cue::focus);
        else if (!input.nav_repeat)
            sound_.play(hui::audio::Cue::error);
    }
    sound_.update(dt);
    was_watching_ = s_.watching;

    dropdown_t_.target = s_.dropdown_open ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
    dropdown_t_.update(dt, 22.0f);
    sign_in_t_.target = s_.sign_in_open ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
    sign_in_t_.update(dt, 18.0f);
    toast_t_.target = s_.toast_open ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
    toast_t_.update(dt, 18.0f);
    // A new screen (page, detail, search, player) restarts the fade-in. It is
    // visual only: input is never blocked, and a fast second navigation just
    // restarts it instead of queuing.
    const unsigned key = static_cast<unsigned>(screen_) * 64u + static_cast<unsigned>(page_) +
                         (s_.watching ? 4096u : 0u);
    if (key != screen_key_)
    {
        screen_key_ = key;
        screen_t_.start(kScreenFade);
    }
    screen_t_.update(dt);
}

void Shell::draw(hui::gfx::DrawList &bg, hui::gfx::DrawList &fg, hui::gfx::BackdropSpec &backdrop) const
{
    // The first moments: the page's light and the mark, nothing else (the pages under it are not drawn).
    if (boot_out_ < 0.0f)
    {
        apply_page_backdrop(backdrop, boot_t_);
        draw_boot(bg, env_, boot_t_, 0.0f);
        return;
    }
    if (s_.watching)
    {
        if (s_.launch_open)
        {
            backdrop.mode = hui::gfx::BackdropMode::none;
            draw_launch(bg, fg, env_, s_.launch, watch_t_, std::min(1.0f, watch_t_ * 3.0f));
        }
        else
        {
            backdrop.mode = hui::gfx::BackdropMode::none;
            bg.rounded_rect({0, 0, theme::kScreenW, theme::kScreenH}, 0, hui::gfx::Color::rgb(0x000000));
            if (s_.video_texture != 0)
            {
                float w = static_cast<float>(s_.video_w), h = static_cast<float>(s_.video_h);
                std::uint32_t draw_tex = s_.video_texture;
                // FSR1/Anime4K upscaling: when the source is below display res
                // and the user enabled an upscaler, run EASU (+RCAS) to the
                // display size first. The upscaler does raw GL here (before any
                // bg.* calls for this frame's video); it saves/restores the
                // viewport and FBO binding (hui rebinds its program/VAO/textures
                // before drawing). Fail-closed: any issue draws the source directly.
                if (!s_.upscaler_tried_)
                {
                    s_.upscaler_tried_ = true;
                    s_.upscaler_ok_ = s_.upscaler_.ensure();
                }
                UpscaleMode um = s_.upscaler_mode();
                if (s_.upscaler_ok_ && um != UpscaleMode::Off &&
                    s_.video_w < s_.display_width() && s_.video_h < s_.display_height() &&
                    s_.video_w > 0 && s_.video_h > 0)
                {
                    int uw = 0, uh = 0;
                    // Aspect-fit the upscale target into the display. EASU assumes
                    // 1:1 vUV mapping, so a non-16:9 source upscaled to the full
                    // 16:9 display would stretch. Fit by aspect instead.
                    const int dw = s_.display_width(), dh = s_.display_height();
                    const double scale = std::min(double(dw) / s_.video_w, double(dh) / s_.video_h);
                    const int dst_w = int(s_.video_w * scale + 0.5);
                    const int dst_h = int(s_.video_h * scale + 0.5);
                    std::uint32_t up = s_.upscaler_.upscale(
                        s_.video_texture, s_.video_w, s_.video_h,
                        dst_w, dst_h, um,
                        s_.upscaler_sharpness(), uw, uh);
                    if (up != 0)
                    {
                        draw_tex = up;
                        w = static_cast<float>(uw);
                        h = static_cast<float>(uh);
                    }
                }
                if (w > 0.0f && h > 0.0f)
                {
                    // Fit: whole picture, scale-down-only (Tier-4b). Fill: crop
                    // the edges to cover the screen; the framebuffer clips the
                    // overdrawn quad, UVs stay correct, so no scissor is needed.
                    const bool fill = s_.aspect_mode() == "fill";
                    const float scale = fill ? std::max(theme::kScreenW / w, theme::kScreenH / h)
                                             : std::min({1.0f, theme::kScreenW / w, theme::kScreenH / h});
                    w *= scale;
                    h *= scale;
                }
                bg.image(draw_tex, {(theme::kScreenW - w) * 0.5f, (theme::kScreenH - h) * 0.5f, w, h},
                         hui::gfx::kFullUv, hui::gfx::Color::rgb(0xffffff));
            }
            player_ui_.draw(bg, fg);
        }
    }
    else if (screen_ == Screen::detail)
    {
        // A soft fade-in on screen changes: 0.7 -> 1.0 over ~180ms. Subtle
        // enough to feel instant, smooth enough to hide the hard cut.
        const float fade = screen_fade();
        bg.push_opacity(fade);
        fg.push_opacity(fade);
        detail_.draw(bg, fg, backdrop);
        fg.pop_opacity();
        bg.pop_opacity();
    }
    else if (screen_ == Screen::search)
    {
        const float fade = screen_fade();
        bg.push_opacity(fade);
        fg.push_opacity(fade);
        search_board_.draw(bg, fg, backdrop);
        fg.pop_opacity();
        bg.pop_opacity();
    }
    else
    {
        const float fade = screen_fade();
        bg.push_opacity(fade);
        fg.push_opacity(fade);
        switch (page_)
        {
        case 0:
            board_.draw(bg, fg, backdrop);
            break;
        case 1:
            discover_.draw(bg, fg, backdrop);
            break;
        case 2:
            library_.draw(bg, fg, backdrop);
            break;
        case 3:
            addons_.draw(bg, fg, backdrop);
            break;
        default:
            settings_.draw(bg, fg, backdrop);
            break;
        }
        fg.pop_opacity();
        bg.pop_opacity();
    }
    if (dropdown_t_.value > 0.01f)
        draw_dropdown(bg, fg, env_, s_.dropdown, dropdown_t_.value);
    if (sign_in_t_.value > 0.01f)
        draw_sign_in(bg, fg, env_, s_.sign_in, clock_, sign_in_t_.value);
    if (toast_t_.value > 0.01f)
    {
        // During playback the bottom-center toast would sit on the subtitles
        // (baseline ~960) and the controls row (y 978-1042): lift it to the
        // top, clear of the title block (ends ~y 108) and the cards below.
        const float toast_y = (s_.watching && !s_.launch_open) ? 140.0f : 936.0f;
        draw_toast(fg, env_, s_.toast, toast_t_.value, toast_y);
    }
    if (boot_out_ < kBootOut)
        draw_boot(bg, env_, boot_t_, boot_out_ < 0.0f ? 0.0f : boot_out_ / kBootOut);
}

} // namespace sx


===== FILE: ui/shell.hpp =====
// Which screen is showing and how the controller moves between them: the five
// pages of the rail, search results, a title's page, and the launch screen and
// player over all of those, with the pop-ups on top. It decides what a button
// means, tells the session when something was chosen, and tells the screens
// when the session changed what they show.

#pragma once

#include "addons.hpp"
#include "board.hpp"
#include "detail.hpp"
#include "discover.hpp"
#include "library.hpp"
#include "overlays.hpp"
#include "player.hpp"
#include "settings.hpp"
#include "../gl/session.hpp"
#include "../gl/ui_sound.hpp"

namespace sx
{

class Shell
{
  public:
    Shell(const Env &env, Session &session);

    void update(const hui::InputFrame &input, float dt);
    // Loads the interface sounds from <dir>/glass and opens the audio port.
    void start_sound(const std::string &dir)
    {
        sound_.start(dir);
    }
    // The boot animation has been running for this long already (frames drawn while the app loaded).
    void start_boot_at(float seconds)
    {
        boot_t_ = seconds;
    }
    // Glass Phase 1: bg is the page content (artwork, text, cards); fg is the
    // frosted-glass panels, drawn after renderer.glass() blurs bg.
    void draw(hui::gfx::DrawList &bg, hui::gfx::DrawList &fg, hui::gfx::BackdropSpec &backdrop) const;

    // Where the cursor is, for the session to load artwork around it.
    Session::Cursor cursor() const;

  private:
    enum class Screen
    {
        page,
        search,
        detail,
    };

    void go_page(int page);
    void open_title(const Title &title, const std::string &streams_video = std::string());
    void route_page(const hui::InputFrame &input, float dt);
    void route_search(const hui::InputFrame &input, float dt);
    void route_detail(const hui::InputFrame &input, float dt);
    void apply_changes();
    void leave_title();

    const Env &env_;
    Session &s_;
    Board board_;
    Board search_board_;
    Discover discover_;
    Library library_;
    Addons addons_;
    Settings settings_;
    Detail detail_;
    PlayerUi player_ui_;
    Screen screen_ = Screen::page;
    Screen came_from_ = Screen::page; // what a title's page goes back to
    int page_ = 0;
    float clock_ = 0.0f;
    UiSound sound_;
    // Where the focus is, as one number, to hear whether a button moved it.
    unsigned focus_signature();
    unsigned view_key() const;
    float boot_t_ = 0.0f;    // seconds since the app started
    float boot_out_ = -1.0f; // -1 while the first screen is being got ready, then seconds since it was
    static constexpr float kBootOut = 0.5f; // how long the mark takes to lift away
    float watch_t_ = 0.0f;   // seconds since the launch screen came up
    float typing_hold_ = 0.0f; // the keyboard's last button presses are not ours
    bool was_watching_ = false;
    // The pop-ups fade in and out.
    hui::tween::Spring dropdown_t_, sign_in_t_, toast_t_;
    // Screen changes get a subtle fade-in (never blocks input; restarts if the
    // user navigates fast, so animations never queue).
    hui::tween::Timer screen_t_;
    unsigned screen_key_ = 0;
    static constexpr float kScreenFade = 0.18f; // seconds: quick, not flashy
    // 0.7 -> 1.0 while the timer runs; 1.0 when it never started (first frame).
    float screen_fade() const
    {
        const float p = screen_t_.running ? hui::tween::cubic_out(screen_t_.progress()) : 1.0f;
        return 0.7f + 0.3f * p;
    }
};

} // namespace sx


===== FILE: ui/text_cache.cpp =====
#include "text_cache.hpp"

namespace sx
{

namespace
{

CachedText::Entry &entry_for(CachedText &cache, const hui::gfx::Font *font, std::string_view text, float size,
                             float width, bool wrap)
{
    for (CachedText::Entry &e : cache.entries)
    {
        if (e.used && e.font == font && e.wrap == wrap && e.size == size && e.width == width && e.text == text)
            return e;
    }
    // Miss: reuse the first free slot, else the oldest (slot 0).
    for (CachedText::Entry &e : cache.entries)
    {
        if (!e.used)
        {
            e.used = true;
            e.done = false;
            e.font = font;
            e.text = std::string(text);
            e.size = size;
            e.width = width;
            e.wrap = wrap;
            return e;
        }
    }
    CachedText::Entry &e = cache.entries[0];
    e.done = false;
    e.font = font;
    e.text = std::string(text);
    e.size = size;
    e.width = width;
    e.wrap = wrap;
    e.fitted.clear();
    e.lines.clear();
    return e;
}

} // namespace

const std::string &cached_fit(CachedText &cache, const hui::gfx::Font *font, std::string_view text, float size,
                              float width)
{
    CachedText::Entry &e = entry_for(cache, font, text, size, width, false);
    if (!e.done)
    {
        e.fitted = text.empty() ? std::string() : font->fit(text, size, width);
        e.done = true;
    }
    return e.fitted;
}

const std::vector<std::string> &cached_wrap(CachedText &cache, const hui::gfx::Font *font, std::string_view text,
                                            float size, float width)
{
    CachedText::Entry &e = entry_for(cache, font, text, size, width, true);
    if (!e.done)
    {
        e.lines = text.empty() ? std::vector<std::string>() : font->wrap(text, size, width);
        e.done = true;
    }
    return e.lines;
}

// Same as hui::ui::paragraph: word-wrapped from the first baseline at y, at
// most max_lines (the last one ends in an ellipsis if text remains). Only the
// wrap/fit go through the cache.
float cached_paragraph(hui::gfx::DrawList &list, CachedText &cache, const hui::ui::FontRef &font,
                       std::string_view value, float x, float y, float size, float width, float line_height,
                       hui::gfx::Color color, int max_lines, hui::gfx::Align align)
{
    // Copy: the fit below can evict the wrap entry this reference points at.
    const std::vector<std::string> lines = cached_wrap(cache, font.font, value, size, width);
    int drawn = 0;
    for (const std::string &line : lines)
    {
        const bool last =
            drawn + 1 == max_lines && lines.size() > static_cast<std::size_t>(max_lines);
        if (last)
            hui::ui::text(list, font, cached_fit(cache, font.font, line + " \xE2\x80\xA6", size, width), x, y,
                          size, color, align);
        else
            hui::ui::text(list, font, line, x, y, size, color, align);
        y += line_height;
        if (++drawn >= max_lines)
            break;
    }
    return y;
}

} // namespace sx


===== FILE: ui/text_cache.hpp =====
#pragma once

// Per-item cache for laid-out text. wrap()/fit() shape through HarfBuzz, so
// calling them every frame for the same string is pure waste; these return
// identical results and only re-layout when the (font, text, size, width)
// key changes. Refs stay valid: entries live in a fixed array.

#include <array>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

#include "gfx/font.hpp"
#include "gfx/draw_list.hpp"
#include "ui/fonts.hpp"

namespace sx
{

struct CachedText
{
    struct Entry
    {
        const void *font = nullptr;
        std::string text;
        float size = 0.0f, width = 0.0f;
        bool wrap = false; // true: lines is valid; false: fitted is valid
        bool used = false;
        bool done = false; // the value was laid out for the current key
        std::string fitted;
        std::vector<std::string> lines;
    };
    // Twelve slots: the hero lays out ~7 distinct keys per frame, the detail
    // page ~10 (a smaller cache would re-lay-out every frame there).
    std::array<Entry, 12> entries;
};

// font->fit() with the result cached.
const std::string &cached_fit(CachedText &cache, const hui::gfx::Font *font, std::string_view text, float size,
                              float width);
// font->wrap() with the result cached.
const std::vector<std::string> &cached_wrap(CachedText &cache, const hui::gfx::Font *font, std::string_view text,
                                            float size, float width);
// hui::ui::paragraph() with the wrap/fit cached: pixel-identical output.
float cached_paragraph(hui::gfx::DrawList &list, CachedText &cache, const hui::ui::FontRef &font,
                       std::string_view value, float x, float y, float size, float width, float line_height,
                       hui::gfx::Color color, int max_lines = 99, hui::gfx::Align align = hui::gfx::Align::left);

} // namespace sx

===== FILE: ui/theme.hpp =====
// Stremio on PS5: the design tokens every screen draws with.
//
// The identity is Stremio's: near-black ground with violet light, the violet
// accent, white focus. Everything is laid out on the toolkit's 1920 x 1080
// virtual canvas (third_party/hui), which it scales to 1080p, 1440p or 4K.

#pragma once

#include "gfx/draw_list.hpp"

namespace sx::theme
{

using hui::gfx::Color;

inline const Color ground = Color::rgb(0x0c0b11);       // the page
inline const Color accent = Color::rgb(0x7b5bf5);       // Stremio violet
inline const Color accent_soft = Color::rgb(0xa58bff);  // violet text on dark
inline const Color accent_deep = Color::rgb(0x3a2a8f);  // the glow behind the page
inline const Color ink = Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.94f);   // titles
inline const Color ink_2 = Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.72f); // body text
inline const Color ink_3 = Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.52f); // labels, 5.5:1 on the ground
inline const Color surface = Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.07f);
inline const Color surface_hi = Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.13f);
inline const Color hairline = Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.10f);
inline const Color watched = Color::rgb(0x22b365);
inline const Color imdb = Color::rgb(0xf5c518);

// Glass Phase 1: frosted-glass surfaces. The visual language (translucent
// dark fill + 1px inner rim light + soft drop shadow over a blurred backdrop)
// is inspired by Fxy6969's "liquid-glass" Stremio theme; the blur itself comes
// from the toolkit's DrawList::glass over renderer.glass_texture().
inline const Color glass_fill = Color::rgb(0x464646, 0.22f);    // dark glass pill fill
inline const Color glass_deep = Color::rgb(0x000000, 0.41f);    // Watch-button glass
inline const Color glass_hair = Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.10f);    // 1px rim
inline const Color glass_hair_hi = Color::rgb(0xffffff, 0.20f);// active/focus rim
// accent stays violet: Stremio's identity, unlike the web theme's white flip.

// Layout (virtual pixels).
constexpr float kRailWidth = 140.0f;  // the left navigation
constexpr float kLeft = 190.0f;       // where page content starts
constexpr float kRight = 1860.0f;     // where it ends
constexpr float kScreenW = 1920.0f;
constexpr float kScreenH = 1080.0f;

// Type sizes: nothing a person has to read is under 20.
constexpr float kLabel = 20.0f;
constexpr float kBody = 24.0f;
constexpr float kSubtitle = 28.0f;
constexpr float kHeading = 32.0f;

} // namespace sx::theme
